# Unit 20 linguistic and owned-edition mapping packet

This packet prepares review. It records an unsigned structural mapping proposal. It does not record a qualified review, an approved edition alignment, or an install decision.

The owned-edition mapping worksheet separates observed edition facts, the proposed lesson and printed page range, unresolved edition questions, and matters that still require qualified linguistic review. ISBN, owned-copy verification, variance, reviewer identity, qualification, date, and approval stay blank. The disposition remains PENDING_CURRICULUM_REVIEWER.

Unit 20 remains in development. Its readiness is content-complete and review-pending. The Lesson 20 relationship is a proposed, unsigned mapping. It is not an approved edition alignment.

`unitContentReviewDigest` binds this unit's instructional curriculum. It covers this unit and the reachable instructional records, relationships, shared dependencies, sources, rights, assets, and advancement rule. It does not cover any other unit. Recording a review status, reviewer signature, mapping approval, install state, or readiness does not change it.

`mappingPackageDigest` binds the proposed edition-mapping facts for Unit 20: edition identity, lesson, printed page range, topic description, observation source, ISBN observation state, and the pending disposition. Reviewer identity, qualification, date, notes, correction notes, and signature stay outside that digest.

`packContentReviewDigest` is the repository aggregate of instructional curriculum. It is not an approval of this unit, and it is not part of the review-package digest.

`reviewPackageDigest` identifies this complete pending review package. It binds the ordered instructional records and the mapping-package digest. Changing a proposed page range or topic changes the mapping-package digest and therefore this package digest.

`repositorySourceDigest` binds repository and release inputs. Recording an approval later changes that repository hash. That change does not replace the signed content-review digest or the mapping-package digest.

The marker scan used by this package detects only a fixed list of known prohibited mapping-input markers. It is not comprehensive copyright detection and cannot recognize every copy or close paraphrase of protected material.

Мальчик отошёл от незнакомца
The boy stepped back from the stranger.
Which translation covers only the shown sentence?
Мальчик отошёл от незнакомца means the boy stepped back from the stranger. No candy offer is in the Russian.
The Russian sentence contains only the boy stepping back from the stranger.
They will leave Vladivostok in a month.
The ball flew onto the roof of the house.
They will leave Vladivostok in a month. is a different motion sentence.
The ball flew onto the roof of the house. is a different motion sentence.

Он пошёл к другу.
he set off toward a friend
Which reading matches the perfective setting-out verb?
пошёл presents setting out toward a friend. A synonym of that same reading is not a second option.
The perfective пошёл presents the start of the trip, one reading.
he was already on the way for a long time
he refused to go
he was already on the way for a long time is an imperfective duration reading.
he refused to go contradicts the motion verb.

recordType=original-synthesis
sourceUrl=urn:russian-companion:content-audit-research:2026-10-08
sourceRevision=content-audit-2026-10-08
sourceRecordId=ca-corrected-capability:cap-u20-motion-po-setting-out
originalSourceText=Pending corrected research applications. No linguistic or owner approval is recorded.
References: retained original source bindings in this unit; the independent content-audit register; Gramota dictionary and orthography; Litnevskaya, verbal adverbs; Rusgram, verbal adverbs.
https://gramota.ru/poisk?query=быстрый&mode=slovari
https://gramota.ru/biblioteka/spravochniki/russkij-yazyk-kratkij-teoreticheskij-kurs-dlya-shkolnikov/deeprichastie
Proposed Unit 20 uses admitted motion lines for direction, foot and transport, carrying and leading, setting out, and selected prefixed paths. Qualified review is pending.
идти́ — to go, walk. е́хать — to go by vehicle, ride. 
unidirectional
нести́ — to carry. вести́ — to lead. 
Он пошёл к другу. — beginning of a movement. Он шёл к другу. — He went to a friend's place. 
Они у́летят из Владивостока через месяц. — They will leave Vladivostok in a month. Мяч залетел на крышу дома. — The ball flew onto the roof of the house. 
идти́
to go, walk
е́хать
to go by vehicle, ride
нести́
to carry
вести́
to lead
Он пошёл к другу.
beginning of a movement
Он шёл к другу.
He went to a friend's place
Они у́летят из Владивостока через месяц.
They will leave Vladivostok in a month.
Мяч залетел на крышу дома.
The ball flew onto the roof of the house.
he set off toward a friend
Which reading matches the perfective setting-out verb?
Он пошёл к другу. means he set off toward a friend: пошёл presents the start of the journey, and к другу gives its destination.
he was already on the way for a long time
he refused to go
he was already on the way for a long time is an imperfective duration reading.
he refused to go contradicts the motion verb.

https://gramota.ru/biblioteka/spravochniki/russkij-yazyk-kratkij-teoreticheskij-kurs-dlya-shkolnikov/deeprichastie
Proposed Unit 20 uses admitted motion lines for direction, foot and transport, carrying and leading, setting out, and selected prefixed paths. Qualified review is pending.
идти́ — to go, walk. е́хать — to go by vehicle, ride. 
unidirectional
нести́ — to carry. вести́ — to lead. 
Он пошёл к другу. — beginning of a movement. Он шёл к другу. — He went to a friend's place. 
Они у́летят из Владивостока через месяц. — They will leave Vladivostok in a month. Мяч залетел на крышу дома. — The ball flew onto the roof of the house. 
идти́
to go, walk
е́хать
to go by vehicle, ride
нести́
to carry
вести́
to lead
Он пошёл к другу.
beginning of a movement
Он шёл к другу.
He went to a friend's place
Они у́летят из Владивостока через месяц.
They will leave Vladivostok in a month.
Мяч залетел на крышу дома.
The ball flew onto the roof of the house.
he set off toward a friend
Which reading matches the perfective setting-out verb?
Он пошёл к другу. means he set off toward a friend: пошёл presents the start of the journey, and к другу gives its destination.
he was already on the way for a long time
he refused to go
he was already on the way for a long time is an imperfective duration reading.
he refused to go contradicts the motion verb.

Мяч залетел на крышу дома.
The ball flew onto the roof of the house.
he set off toward a friend
Which reading matches the perfective setting-out verb?
Он пошёл к другу. means he set off toward a friend: пошёл presents the start of the journey, and к другу gives its destination.
he was already on the way for a long time
he refused to go
he was already on the way for a long time is an imperfective duration reading.
he refused to go contradicts the motion verb.
shippedText=he refused to go contradicts the motion verb.

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:! English !! unidirectional !! multidirectional
|- style="vertical-align: top;"

originalSourceText=! English !! unidirectional !! multidirectional
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to run || бежа́ть || бе́гать
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to wander || брести́ || броди́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to convey, transport || везти́ || вози́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to lead || вести́ || води́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to drive, chase || гна́ть || гоня́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to go by vehicle, ride || е́хать || е́здить
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to go, walk || идти́ || ходи́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to roll || кати́ть || ката́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to climb || ле́зть || ла́зить (ла́зать)
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to fly || лете́ть || лета́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to carry || нести́ || носи́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to swim, float || плы́ть || пла́вать
|- style="vertical-align: top;"

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:The addition of the prefix по- to a unidirectional verb of motion makes the verb
originalSourceText=The addition of the prefix по- to a unidirectional verb of motion makes the verb perfective, denoting the beginning of a movement, i.e. 'setting out'. These perfectives imply that the agent has not yet returned at the moment of speech, e.g.,<ref name="Wade2011">{{cite book|last=Wade|first=Terence|title=A Comprehensive Russian Grammar|date=2011|publisher=Blackwell Publishers Ltd.|location=Oxford|edition=2}}</ref>{{rp|353–355}}

{{ordered list|type=lower-latin
 | He went to a friend's place (and has not returned; unidirectional perfective).<br />Он '''по'''шёл к другу.<br />
''Compare with:''
 | He was on his way to a friend's place (unidirectional imperfective).<br />Он шёл к другу.
shippedText=beginning of a movement
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.
sourceHash=3dfba3080fe3be29169e2b38f020dc1ed2af4e0a0b35f2ded1b9fb5bb21e5903

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:The addition of the prefix по- to a unidirectional verb of motion makes the verb
originalSourceText=The addition of the prefix по- to a unidirectional verb of motion makes the verb perfective, denoting the beginning of a movement, i.e. 'setting out'. These perfectives imply that the agent has not yet returned at the moment of speech, e.g.,<ref name="Wade2011">{{cite book|last=Wade|first=Terence|title=A Comprehensive Russian Grammar|date=2011|publisher=Blackwell Publishers Ltd.|location=Oxford|edition=2}}</ref>{{rp|353–355}}

{{ordered list|type=lower-latin
 | He went to a friend's place (and has not returned; unidirectional perfective).<br />Он '''по'''шёл к другу.<br />
''Compare with:''
 | He was on his way to a friend's place (unidirectional imperfective).<br />Он шёл к другу.
shippedText=He went to a friend's place
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.

These perfectives imply that the agent has not yet returned at the moment of speech, e.g.,<ref name="Wade2011">{{cite book|last=Wade|first=Terence|title=A Comprehensive Russian Grammar|date=2011|publisher=Blackwell Publishers Ltd.|location=Oxford|edition=2}}</ref>{{rp|353–355}}

{{ordered list|type=lower-latin

''Compare with:''

### PROVENANCE `prov-ex-u20-motion-6-en`

- Objectives: obj-u20-motion
- Sessions: u20-s-orient, u20-s-motion
- Source: src-wikipedia-russian-grammar-1369617627

- Content hash: 7793b207ff9be1e12dd00b527693725b174846572b2ac87ed44722458e580bb4

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:They will leave Vladivostok in a month.<br />Они '''у'''летят из Владивостока че
originalSourceText=They will leave Vladivostok in a month.<br />Они '''у'''летят из Владивостока через месяц.<br />Where is Igor? He already left.<br />Где Игорь? Он уже '''у'''шёл.
| в / на + {{abbr|acc.|accusative}}<br />к + {{abbr|dat.|dative}}<br />из / с / от + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| под-, подо-<br />Approach<br />Antonym: от-
| He approached the girl to ask for her number.<br />Он '''подо'''шёл к девушке, чтобы спросить её номер.
''Other:''
'''Под'''везти – give someone a lift, e.g.:
: He took me (as far as) downtown.
: Он '''под'''вёз меня до центра.
| к + {{abbr|dat.|dative}}<br />до + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| от-, ото-<br />Withdrawal a short distance away<br />Antonym: под-
| The boy stepped back from the stranger who had offered him candy.<br />Мальчик '''ото'''шёл от незнакомца, который предложил ему конфеты.
''Other:''
With transitive verbs, delivering or dropping something off (agent does not remain), e.g.:
: I'll drop the book off at the library, then come.<br />Я '''от'''несу книги в библиотеку, потом приду.
| от + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| до-<br />Reaching a limit or destination
| The passengers reached the last station and exited the bus.<br />Пассажиры '''до'''ехали до последней остановки и вышли из автобуса.
''Other:''
Characterizing the duration of a journey, especially when it is long, e.g.:
: We finally reached the dacha.
: Мы наконец '''до'''ехали до дачи.
| до + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| за-<br />Movement behind an object; stopping off on the way
| The old woman walked behind the corner and disappeared.<br />Старушка '''за'''шла за угол и исчезла.
''Other:''
{{ordered list|type=decimal
| Action performed on the way to a destination, e.g.:<br />On the way home I stopped at the store for bread.<br />По дороге домой я '''за'''шла в магазин за хлебом
| A short visit, e.g.:<br />The young man often stops by his mother's place.<br />Молодой человек часто '''за'''ходит к маме.
| Movement deep into something, at a great distance (inside, upwards or downwards), e.g.:<br />The ball flew onto the roof of the house.<br />Мяч '''за'''летел на крышу дома.
shippedText=They will leave Vladivostok in a month.
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.

recordType=instruction
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:! English !! unidirectional !! multidirectional
|- style="vertical-align: top;"

originalSourceText=! English !! unidirectional !! multidirectional
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to run || бежа́ть || бе́гать
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to wander || брести́ || броди́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to convey, transport || везти́ || вози́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to lead || вести́ || води́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to drive, chase || гна́ть || гоня́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to go by vehicle, ride || е́хать || е́здить
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to go, walk || идти́ || ходи́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to roll || кати́ть || ката́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to climb || ле́зть || ла́зить (ла́зать)
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to fly || лете́ть || лета́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to carry || нести́ || носи́ть
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to swim, float || плы́ть || пла́вать
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to crawl || ползти́ || по́лзать
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| to drag || тащи́ть || таска́ть

recordType=label
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:The addition of the prefix по- to a unidirectional verb of motion makes the verb
originalSourceText=The addition of the prefix по- to a unidirectional verb of motion makes the verb perfective, denoting the beginning of a movement, i.e. 'setting out'. These perfectives imply that the agent has not yet returned at the moment of speech, e.g.,<ref name="Wade2011">{{cite book|last=Wade|first=Terence|title=A Comprehensive Russian Grammar|date=2011|publisher=Blackwell Publishers Ltd.|location=Oxford|edition=2}}</ref>{{rp|353–355}}

{{ordered list|type=lower-latin
 | He went to a friend's place (and has not returned; unidirectional perfective).<br />Он '''по'''шёл к другу.<br />
''Compare with:''
 | He was on his way to a friend's place (unidirectional imperfective).<br />Он шёл к другу.
shippedText=setting out
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.
sourceHash=3dfba3080fe3be29169e2b38f020dc1ed2af4e0a0b35f2ded1b9fb5bb21e5903

These perfectives imply that the agent has not yet returned at the moment of speech, e.g.,<ref name="Wade2011">{{cite book|last=Wade|first=Terence|title=A Comprehensive Russian Grammar|date=2011|publisher=Blackwell Publishers Ltd.|location=Oxford|edition=2}}</ref>{{rp|353–355}}

 | He went to a friend's place (and has not returned; unidirectional perfective).<br />Он '''по'''шёл к другу.<br />
''Compare with:''
 | He was on his way to a friend's place (unidirectional imperfective).<br />Он шёл к другу.

recordType=label
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar:They will leave Vladivostok in a month.<br />Они '''у'''летят из Владивостока че
originalSourceText=They will leave Vladivostok in a month.<br />Они '''у'''летят из Владивостока через месяц.<br />Where is Igor? He already left.<br />Где Игорь? Он уже '''у'''шёл.
| в / на + {{abbr|acc.|accusative}}<br />к + {{abbr|dat.|dative}}<br />из / с / от + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| под-, подо-<br />Approach<br />Antonym: от-
| He approached the girl to ask for her number.<br />Он '''подо'''шёл к девушке, чтобы спросить её номер.
''Other:''
'''Под'''везти – give someone a lift, e.g.:
: He took me (as far as) downtown.
: Он '''под'''вёз меня до центра.
| к + {{abbr|dat.|dative}}<br />до + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| от-, ото-<br />Withdrawal a short distance away<br />Antonym: под-
| The boy stepped back from the stranger who had offered him candy.<br />Мальчик '''ото'''шёл от незнакомца, который предложил ему конфеты.
''Other:''
With transitive verbs, delivering or dropping something off (agent does not remain), e.g.:
: I'll drop the book off at the library, then come.<br />Я '''от'''несу книги в библиотеку, потом приду.
| от + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| до-<br />Reaching a limit or destination
| The passengers reached the last station and exited the bus.<br />Пассажиры '''до'''ехали до последней остановки и вышли из автобуса.
''Other:''
Characterizing the duration of a journey, especially when it is long, e.g.:
: We finally reached the dacha.
: Мы наконец '''до'''ехали до дачи.
| до + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| за-<br />Movement behind an object; stopping off on the way
| The old woman walked behind the corner and disappeared.<br />Старушка '''за'''шла за угол и исчезла.
''Other:''
{{ordered list|type=decimal
| Action performed on the way to a destination, e.g.:<br />On the way home I stopped at the store for bread.<br />По дороге домой я '''за'''шла в магазин за хлебом
| A short visit, e.g.:<br />The young man often stops by his mother's place.<br />Молодой человек часто '''за'''ходит к маме.
| Movement deep into something, at a great distance (inside, upwards or downwards), e.g.:<br />The ball flew onto the roof of the house.<br />Мяч '''за'''летел на крышу дома.
shippedText=The boy stepped back from the stranger who had offered him candy.
transformation=selection
licenseId=CC BY-SA 4.0
licenseUrl=https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/
attribution=Wikipedia contributors, CC BY-SA 4.0, via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. Page history for oldid 1369617627 identifies the contributors.
shareAlikeTreatment=Adapted curriculum data, selection, and arrangement are distributed under CC BY-SA 4.0. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside that statement. No audio is relicensed because this unit ships no audio.

|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| под-, подо-<br />Approach<br />Antonym: от-
| He approached the girl to ask for her number.<br />Он '''подо'''шёл к девушке, чтобы спросить её номер.
''Other:''
'''Под'''везти – give someone a lift, e.g.:
: He took me (as far as) downtown.
: Он '''под'''вёз меня до центра.
| к + {{abbr|dat.|dative}}<br />до + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| от-, ото-<br />Withdrawal a short distance away<br />Antonym: под-
| The boy stepped back from the stranger who had offered him candy.<br />Мальчик '''ото'''шёл от незнакомца, который предложил ему конфеты.
''Other:''
With transitive verbs, delivering or dropping something off (agent does not remain), e.g.:
: I'll drop the book off at the library, then come.<br />Я '''от'''несу книги в библиотеку, потом приду.
| от + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| до-<br />Reaching a limit or destination
| The passengers reached the last station and exited the bus.<br />Пассажиры '''до'''ехали до последней остановки и вышли из автобуса.
''Other:''
Characterizing the duration of a journey, especially when it is long, e.g.:
: We finally reached the dacha.
: Мы наконец '''до'''ехали до дачи.
| до + {{abbr|gen.|genitive}}
|- style="vertical-align: top;"
| за-<br />Movement behind an object; stopping off on the way
| The old woman walked behind the corner and disappeared.<br />Старушка '''за'''шла за угол и исчезла.
''Other:''
{{ordered list|type=decimal
| Action performed on the way to a destination, e.g.:<br />On the way home I stopped at the store for bread.<br />По дороге домой я '''за'''шла в магазин за хлебом
| A short visit, e.g.:<br />The young man often stops by his mother's place.<br />Молодой человек часто '''за'''ходит к маме.
| Movement deep into something, at a great distance (inside, upwards or downwards), e.g.:<br />The ball flew onto the roof of the house.<br />Мяч '''за'''летел на крышу дома.
